(2) The "Carrier" stat tile (always reading `carrier_name` / defaulting to "MERCON LOGISTICS") was removed from the Route Overview card — not needed per owner; Distance is now the only stat shown. (3) Gray/tinted fills that read as "AI slop" were replaced with the plain white-card + hairline-border look already established elsewhere (`border-black/[0.06]`/`border-black/[0.08]`, no fill): the Distance tile's `bg-gray-50`, the stops-timeline's `border-gray-100`/`bg-gray-300` dot, and the Documents row's `bg-gray-50/60` were all removed. The GPS-tracker "No tracker" `Badge`'s `bg-gray-50` was deliberately left as-is — semantic badge tints (vs. whole-surface fills) are the one place `UI_GUIDELINES.md` explicitly allows tinted backgrounds. Driver/vehicle assignment display was confirmed already present and correct (owner initially thought it was missing, then retracted). Verified: `tsc -b`/`vite build` clean on web dashboard, `tsc --noEmit` clean on mobile; not yet verified in a running app. Earlier the same day: **This is the single source of truth for "where is the project."*4: Last updated: **2026-08-08** (**Excel/PDF report exports rebuilt on real generator libraries**: the shared `frontend/web-dashboard/src/utils/exportUtils.ts` previously faked both formats — "Excel" was an HTML `<table>` saved with a `.xls` extension (`application/vnd.ms-excel` MIME + MSO XML comments), and "PDF" just opened a new window and called `window.print()` for the user to manually Save-as-PDF. Replaced with `exceljs` (real `.xlsx`, added as a new dependency) and `jspdf`+`jspdf-autotable` (real generated `.pdf`, downloaded directly, no print dialog). Two shared generators added: `exportExcelTable`/`exportPDFTable` (explicit title+headers+rows, used by pages that build a custom column set) and `exportExcel`/`exportPDF` (derives headers from a plain object array, mirroring `downloadCSV`'s existing header/value logic). Both classify each cell (currency/number/date/status-good/status-bad/text) from the header text and value shape to apply brand-colored (`#E8450F`) header bands, zebra striping, bold double-bordered totals rows, right-aligned `SAR #,##0.00` currency formatting, green/red status pill fills, frozen header + autofilter (Excel), and a branded footer with page numbers (PDF). Rewired every existing Excel/PDF export call site: `VehicleListPage.tsx`, `DriverListPage.tsx`, `CustomerDetailsPage.tsx` (customer trip ledger), `CustomReportPage.tsx` (custom trip ledger) for Excel; `TripListPage.tsx` (both the filtered-export dialog and the row-selection bulk action) for PDF — all now produce `.xlsx`/`.pdf` filenames instead of `.xls`. CSV exports were left as-is (already a real, correctly-formatted CSV). Verified: `tsc -b` clean on `web-dashboard`; a Node smoke test (stubbed DOM globals) drove `exportExcelTable` directly and the resulting `.xlsx` was unzipped and confirmed to be a valid OOXML package with real `xl/styles.xml` fills/fonts/borders, `autoFilter`, and merged title/subtitle cells — full in-browser click-through wasn't done this session (local API dev server's `nodemon`→`ts-node` spawn wasn't resolving `ts-node` from `PATH` in the preview harness, a pre-existing local-env issue unrelated to this change; `jsPDF`'s Node smoke test hit an unrelated Node/browser package-export-condition mismatch that doesn't affect the real Vite/browser bundle actually used at runtime). CSV import mapping/template, `exportUtils.ts`, `tripService.ts`/`vehicleService.ts` types, `CreateTripPage.tsx`'s hardcoded default), and the mobile app (`CreateTripScreen.tsx`'s required "Cargo" text input + validation — the only real cargo-entry UI anywhere in the app — plus every display/type site: driver Home/Trips/TripCompleted/Pickup+DeliveryVerification screens, operator TripList/TripDetails screens, `lib/trips.ts`/`lib/operator.ts`, `features/drivers` and `features/dashboard` types/services; `dashboardService.ts`'s Active-Vehicles-carousel `originLabel` — previously mis-sourced from `cargo_type` — now uses the trip的 `ref_id` instead). `TripCardSwiper.tsx`'s `cargoType` was deliberately left alone — it's unrelated hardcoded demo telemetry, never wired to real trips. Docs (`MOBILE_API_SPEC.md`, `final_database_schema.md`) updated to match; `api-contracts.md`'s `cargo_type` reference is a separate Rate Card field, untouched. Separately, **`TripDetailsPage.tsx` was rebuilt from scratch**, not edited in place: while this work was in progress, a separate commit on `main` (`e13cbab`, "revert(trips): remove Trip Details page to rebuild from scratch") deleted the old page entirely — along with the `/trips/:id` route, the `UserChip` component, and simplified `TripLiveMapCard`'s props — because the owner judged the old page's design bad, not just cluttered ("I deleted the previous page because it was shit... if you are creating back in the same design and same things, then that's a problem"). The rebuild keeps the same functional coverage (route overview, live map, stops log with delay logging, documents, customer/driver/vehicle cards with assign/replace, invoice, audit trail) but: the dark `#1C1C2E` "instrument panel" hero card is now a plain white `Card` matching the rest of the app (Customer/Dashboard pages already moved to this style); the cargo tile, the 6-step Draft→...→Completed status pill rail, and both "Edit Manifest" entry points (header button + "Add stops in Edit Manifest" link) are gone; and the **Financials/Settlement and Extra Driver Payment cards were cut entirely** per owner request (not just restyled) — their modals (`PostTripSettlementModal`, the cash-payout `ConfirmModal`), state, and mutations were removed from the page. `router.tsx`'s `/trips/:id` route and `UserChip.tsx` were restored (both needed by the rebuilt page); `tripService.ts` regained the `replaceDriver` call and the `Trip`/`TripStop` fields (`created_by`, `updated_by`, `updatedAt`, `vehicle.capacity_kg`, `vehicle.icces_device_id`, `stop.delay_logged_by`) that the deletion commit had trimmed along with the old page, since the rebuilt page still uses them for the audit trail, GPS-tracker badge, and delay "logged by" chip; the `TripLiveMapCard` call site was updated to that commit's simplified prop interface (just `tripId`/`refId`/pickup+dropoff lat-lng, no more `status`/`vehiclePlate`/`hasTracker`/`lastLat`/`lastLng`/`pickupName`/`dropoffName`). `CustomerDetailsPage.tsx` and `DashboardPage.tsx` had their own independent conflicting redesigns from the same period (both had already moved their trip tables onto the `DataTable` component) — those were kept as-is, just with their `Cargo Type` columns dropped. Pickup/dropoff address display needed no change: `TripStop.location_name` is captured via the Create Trip form's Nominatim address search and was already preferred over raw lat/lng, with coordinates only a fallback for stops never given a name (e.g. bulk-imported trips). Earlier the same day: **Trips KPI cards redesigned as clean stat tiles**: `KpiCard.tsx` rewritten — the sharp `rounded-none` "instrument panel" card (corner ticks, hairline accent rule, mono fonts, and a different gadget crammed into each card: semi-circle arc gauge, animated fake-74% pulse bar, donut ring, mini stage boxes) was replaced with one quiet tile that follows `UI_GUIDELINES.md`: `rounded-2xl`, `border-black/[0.06]` + `shadow-sm`, 10px uppercase muted label, 28px bold sans value, tinted rounded-xl icon chip, optional trend pill, and a single restrained footer visual per data shape (slim segmented distribution bar + dot legend, thin single-hue completion meter, live-pulse text row, inline stage counts, or a sparkline — the always-on fake `defaultChartData` sparkline was removed; sparkline now renders only when real `chartData` is passed). All existing props kept so the ~15 other pages using `KpiCard` compile unchanged; the `SemiCircleArcGauge` component was deleted (the `semiCircleGauge` prop now renders as the segmented bar). On the Trips page itself: meaningless decorative trend chips ("↑ All Operations", "→ Scheduled") removed, and TOTAL TRIPS' hardcoded fake sparkline data replaced with the real Completed/In-Transit/Queue status breakdown. `tsc --noEmit` clean; not verified in-browser this session. Earlier that day: **Drivers list actions reworked**: replaced the `Actions` dropdown trigger on the Drivers list table with inline action icon buttons (View, Quick Status, Docs, Edit, Delete) matching the style and layout pattern used in the Trips list table. Earlier that day: **Trips page: Export reworked as a dropdown menu**: replaced the modal-based export flow — which showed generic "All Drivers"/"All Vehicles" `Select`s instead of letting the owner actually pick one, and read as visually inconsistent with the rest of the app per owner feedback — with a `DropdownMenu` matching the pattern already used on Drivers/Invoices/Rate Cards list pages: a CSV/PDF format toggle at the top (emerald `FileSpreadsheet` / rose `FileText` icons), a quick "All Trips" item, "By Status" items (Completed/Delivered, In Transit, Not Completed), and "A Specific Driver"/"A Specific Vehicle" submenus that list actual driver names and plate numbers rather than a scroll-to-find select. Only date-range picking stayed in a dialog (now much smaller — just status + from/to date + the same format toggle), since a date range doesn't fit a menu item. `handleExport` was generalized into a parameterized `runExport(format, opts)` so both the dropdown items and the date-range dialog share one code path. Earlier the same day: **Trips page: CSV import added**: new "Import" button opens a dialog that bulk-creates `Draft`/`Dispatched` trips from an uploaded CSV — customer matched by name, driver/vehicle optionally matched by name/plate (each row independent; a bad row is reported with a reason, not fatal to the batch). Backend: `POST /trips/bulk-import` (`bulkImportTrips` in `tripController.ts`, `bulkImportTripsBody` zod schema, capped at 500 rows/request) reuses the same claim-driver/claim-vehicle transaction logic as `createTrip` but skips the stops requirement — imported trips have no route stops until added later through the normal trip edit UI, since a CSV can't reasonably carry map coordinates. Frontend: `parseCSVFile` added to `exportUtils.ts` (quote-aware CSV parser, header normalization), a downloadable template button, and a results view (imported/failed counts + per-row error list). Also **Export CSV/PDF buttons recolored** per owner request — green (emerald) for CSV, red (rose) for PDF, applied consistently to the Export dialog buttons and the row-selection bulk-action buttons (`DataTable`'s `BulkAction` gained an optional `className` passthrough to support it). Typechecks clean on both workspaces; not verified in-browser (dashboard requires auth, preview pane wasn't rendering this session). Earlier the same day: **Trips page: interactive KPIs + unified export + "New Trip" label**: the "New Trip Draft" button on `/trips` is now just "New Trip" — trip creation was never a separate draft-then-publish flow, `Draft` is simply the `TripStatus` a trip starts in when it's created without both a driver and vehicle assigned (scheduling via `planned_start`/`planned_end`/stop `planned_arrival` was already independent of that status and untouched here). The four KPI cards (`KpiCard.tsx` gained `isActive`/`onStageClick`/`onHealthClick`) are now clickable to filter the trip list by status/route-health/pipeline-stage, with an active ring style; KPI counts were also fixed to compute from the full unfiltered trip set (`allTripsRes`, a new unpaginated query) instead of just the current page. The separate Export CSV/PDF buttons were replaced with one Export dialog (status group, driver, vehicle, date range), and `GET /trips` (`tripController.ts`) plus `tripService.ts` gained a `vehicle_id` filter to support it. Not verified in-browser (dashboard requires auth; another session had the dev server on this port). Earlier the same day: **site-wide `--font-mono` set to JetBrains Mono**: `frontend/web-dashboard` added `@fontsource-variable/jetbrains-mono` (self-hosted, matching the existing `@fontsource-variable/geist`/`inter` pattern) and declared `--font-mono` in `index.css`'s `@theme inline` block, which previously had no override — Tailwind's `font-mono` utility (used across 41 files for plate numbers, ref IDs, timestamps, currency) was falling back to the default OS-monospace stack. `--font-sans` (Geist-based) and the separate "Geist Mono Numbers" digit-only `@font-face` hack were left untouched — out of scope for this change. Verified: `font-mono` utility now resolves to `'JetBrains Mono Variable'` via computed styles, the woff2 loads with a 200 from `node_modules` locally (self-hosted, no CDN), and both `tsc --noEmit` and a full `vite build` pass. Earlier the same day: **Active Freight Trips Carousel rebuilt on shadcn**: `components/trips/TripCardSwiper.tsx` (dashboard) moved off its hand-rolled `overflow-x-auto` + scroll-listener carousel onto the shadcn **`Carousel`** primitive (`components/ui/carousel.tsx`, added — embla-carousel-react, already a dependency), gaining drag, arrow-key nav, snap-accurate index tracking and clickable dot indicators wired through a new `useCarousel()`-consuming `CarouselToolbar` in the card header (`CardAction` slot). Three more shadcn primitives added to support it: **`avatar.tsx`** and **`hover-card.tsx`** (both on `@base-ui/react`, matching the repo's `base-nova` style — hover-card wraps Base UI's `PreviewCard`) and **`skeleton.tsx`**. Each trip card was redesigned: driver tile is now an `Avatar` + `HoverCard` (phone/plate/cargo on hover), vehicle tile a `Tooltip`, the route corridor uses `Separator` with pin/flag icons, the micro-map gained a gradient hover overlay showing km-remaining + ETA, and progress now shows distance done/left. Hover/focus/active animations are shadcn-idiomatic throughout (lift + ring + shadow on the card, a scale-in accent rail, icon transforms, animated dot width) and cards are keyboard-focusable (`Enter`/`Space` to open). Also fixed a latent bug: the progress bar的 `[&>div]` selector was coloring the **track** rather than the indicator, so every bar rendered visually full — it now targets `[data-slot=progress-indicator]` with a gradient fill. Verified by `tsc --noEmit` and a full `vite build`; not verified in-browser (the dashboard is behind login). Earlier: **Trip creation: "assign driver/vehicle later" added**: `POST /trips` no longer requires `driver_id`/`vehicle_id` — a trip created without one or both now lands in `Draft` status instead of `Dispatched` (driver/vehicle claiming and the assignment notification are skipped for whichever is missing). `/trips/:id/dispatch` (`dispatchTrip`) reworked to accept either field independently rather than requiring both, so a Draft trip can have its driver and vehicle assigned in separate calls; it only flips the trip to `Dispatched` once both end up set. `updateTripStatus` gained a guard rejecting a direct Draft→Dispatched status change when the trip still has no driver or vehicle (that path must go through `dispatchTrip`, which does the atomic Available→OnTrip claim) — web's `TripDetailsPage.tsx` mirrors this by hiding the "Mark Dispatched" quick action until both are assigned. Web changes: `CreateTripPage.tsx` (`/trips/new`) step 2 gained "Assign driver later"/"Assign vehicle later" checkboxes that disable the respective picker and relax the step/submit validation; `TripDetailsPage.tsx`'s driver/vehicle cards now show an inline assign-now picker (calling the reworked dispatch endpoint) when a Draft trip is missing one; `Combobox` (`components/ui/combobox.tsx`) gained a `disabled` prop to support the checkbox-disables-picker interaction. Not yet run against a live server for E2E verification — a concurrent session's in-progress `hazmat_flag` removal (`schema.prisma`) had the local API mid-edit and returning 500s on login at the time; both `backend/api-server` and `frontend/web-dashboard` typecheck clean (`tsc --noEmit` / `tsc -b`). **Hazmat removed entirely from the project**: `hazmat_flag` dropped from `Trip` in `schema.prisma` (production data loss on next deploy — owner confirmed), the zod schema, `tripController.createTrip`, both web (`tripService.ts`, `CreateTripPage.tsx`, `TripListPage.tsx` badge + KPI breakdown, `exportUtils.ts` CSV column) and mobile (`operator.ts` types, `TripDetailsScreen.tsx` cargo row) call sites, and the unrelated static "Hazmat Clearance" placeholder badge on `RateCardDetailsPage.tsx`. The IN-TRANSIT KPI's on-schedule/delayed breakdown had been (mis)computed from `hazmat_flag` as a stand-in for "delayed" — there is no real delay signal at the trip level (only per-stop `delay_reason`), so it now reads on-schedule = active-in-transit minus stopped, delayed = 0, until a real delayed-trip metric is built. Earlier the same day: web dashboard **removed the small uppercase "Module" badges next to page titles** — e.g. "Tariff Module" on Rate Cards, "Operations Module" on Trips, "Fleet Module" on Drivers/Vehicles/Maintenance, "Finance Module" on Invoices/Payment Status, "Commercial Module" on Customers, "Platform Module" on User Management, "System Administration" on Settings, "Dashboard Overview" on the dashboard greeting, "Fleet Roster" on Vehicles, "Operations Alert System" on Notifications — across 10 pages, per owner request; the `<h1>` title and the "Scope: ..." subtitle line beneath it were left untouched. Earlier the same day: web dashboard **fixed User Management's missing header/action buttons**: `DashboardLayout` never actually renders its `pageTitle`/`pageSub`/`actions` props — they're accepted but unused in the component body, so any page still passing them (only `UserManagementPage` did) silently got no page header and no action buttons at all, regardless of role. Reworked the page to render its own header row + Admin-only "Add Driver"/"Invite User" buttons inline, matching the pattern every other list page already uses (`DriverListPage`, `CustomerListPage`, etc: icon + title + badge + subtitle row, followed by `Button`s, all inside `children` rather than layout props). Also tightened permissions per owner request: Operators now see Admin/Operator rows only (no Drivers) and are view-only (no create/edit/delete/Add Driver); only Admin gets those actions and the Drivers rows. Backend `userRoutes.ts` split so `GET /users` allows Admin+Operator but `POST/PUT/DELETE /users` require Admin. Verified both roles by injecting a synthetic session client-side (no real credentials used) and reading the rendered page. Earlier the same day: added **`.github/workflows/ci.yml`**: a new hosted-runner (`ubuntu-latest`) CI workflow that runs on every PR into `main` and every push to a non-`main` branch — `npm ci`, `npm run lint -w @mercon/web-dashboard` (oxlint), then `npm run build` (build:types → api-server `tsc` → web-dashboard `tsc -b && vite build`), so a broken build/typecheck is caught before merge instead of failing on the self-hosted deploy runner. This is deliberately a separate workflow from `ci-cd.yml`, not a split of it: `ci-cd.yml` stays a single job because its steps are an ordered, stateful deploy sequence (containers must be healthy before nginx reloads) with no parallelism to gain on a single self-hosted runner — splitting *that* would only add fragile cross-workflow ordering. `ci.yml` is unrelated in trigger (PR/branch, not `push: main`) and runner (hosted, no secrets/prod access needed) so it's a genuinely separate concern, not a job split. Earlier the same day: backend **fixed a brief production outage**: the `ilan` seed commit below had hardcoded `phone: '+966500000003'`, which collided with an existing user's phone (unique constraint) already in the hosted DB; the container's startup chain is `prisma db push && seed.ts && node dist/index.js`, so the thrown seed error meant `node dist/index.js` never ran and `mercon.tech/api/*` 502'd entirely until the fix (drop the hardcoded phone — it's optional on `User`) deployed. Also added a permanent diagnostic step to `ci-cd.yml` that prints `mercon-api` container logs after every deploy so a startup crash is visible in the Actions run instead of a bare 502. Verified: `/api/health` 200 and `POST /api/auth/login` for `ilan`/`ilan1234` returns a valid token with role Admin. Earlier the same day: backend **`prisma/seed.ts` now also upserts a third canonical account, `ilan` (role Admin)**, added at the owner's explicit request alongside the existing `admin`/`operator` upserts — idempotent, own bcrypt hash, existing accounts still never touched. Earlier the same day: web dashboard **`/trips/new` (Create Trip wizard) restyled to match `/drivers/new` and `/vehicles/new`**: the 3-step wizard structure is unchanged (Customer → Driver/Vehicle assignment → Route/geofencing, the last step embeds `LocationPickerMap` so it wasn't collapsed into the single-viewport 2-column layout used on the other two pages), but every hardcoded `slate-*`/`indigo-*`/`rose-*` class was swapped for theme tokens, the step-manifest strip's boxed indigo icon circles were removed in favor of bare icons (matching the orange bare-icon convention already applied to page headers elsewhere), and the error banner now uses the shared `Alert` primitive instead of a hand-rolled rose div. Earlier the same day: web dashboard **User Management (`/settings/users`) opened up to Operator** (previously Admin-only) and turned into a combined "all platform users" view: it now also lists Drivers by querying `/drivers` alongside `/users`, shown read-only (a "View" action links to `/drivers/:id`) since Driver accounts have no login and are still only created/edited through the Drivers module; an "Add Driver" button links to `/drivers/new`. Backend `userRoutes.ts` changed from `authorizeRoles('Admin')` to `authorizeRoles('Admin', 'Operator')` on all `/users` routes; `createUserBody`/`updateUserBody` still restrict `role` to `Admin`/`Operator` only, so Driver-role web accounts remain non-creatable via this form, per the existing Phase-1 rule. `CLAUDE.md`'s "Who uses which app" table updated to match. Earlier the same day: web dashboard **`/vehicles/new` (Add Vehicle) redesigned to match `/drivers/new`**: same 2-column shadcn layout — form card (asset identifier / payload & telematics / optional trailer, separated by `Separator`s) beside a sticky "Registration summary" card with a live 4-item requirements checklist (plate, tractor capacity, trailer config, telematics); the old horizontal preview-strip + narrow single-column card + hardcoded slate/indigo classes were replaced with theme tokens and the shared `Alert` primitive for errors. Earlier the same day: **`/drivers/new` (Add Driver) redesigned** the same way: the full-width preview strip + narrow stacked form became a 2-column shadcn layout — form card (personal profile / commercial licence, separated by `Separator`) beside a sticky "Onboarding summary" card with a live 4-item requirements checklist, so the whole page fits one viewport with no scrolling; errors now render in the new shadcn `Alert` primitive (`components/ui/alert.tsx`, added), the page is a real `<form>` with a submit button, hardcoded slate/indigo classes gave way to theme tokens, a triple Ctrl+Enter submit binding (window listener + two `Btn` shortcuts, all firing the mutation) was reduced to one, and inputs/labels/headings were later sized up a notch (h-11 inputs, text-sm labels) for readability — both pages share that larger sizing. Also that day: **hosted demo-data workflow added**: `.github/workflows/demo-data.yml` loads the ~3,100-trip Delay Report demo dataset into the hosted database and removes it again — `workflow_dispatch` only, two actions (`seed`/`purge`), a typed `SEED`/`PURGE` confirmation checked before anything runs, and a `demo-data` GitHub Environment for a required reviewer. Both scripts run via `docker exec` inside the deployed `mercon-api` container (verified the image ships `scripts/` and `ts-node` — it already runs `npx ts-node prisma/seed.ts` at startup), and `DEMO_RESET` is never passed, so the seeder's destructive path is unreachable from CI. Row counts print before and after, tagged vs total. `ci-cd.yml`, `prisma/seed.ts` and `docker-compose.yml` untouched. Not yet run — the self-hosted runner is the only thing that can reach the production database. Previous session, 2026-08-05: mobile **operator Customers screen rebuilt** on `features/customers` at `/operator/customers` (More → Customers): KPI rail, server-side search/filter, sort, infinite scroll, per-customer trips/revenue/rate-card aggregated from `/trips`+`/invoices`+`/rate-cards`, and a role-gated overflow menu with optimistic suspend/delete; generic `SortDropdown` + `useDebouncedValue` extracted to `shared/`. Also merged in, same day: the **Delay Reporting module** built end to end — a `/reports/delays` page with three views behind one filter bar: the delay log (one row per late arrival, click-through to the trip), an on-time % grid switchable by route/driver/company/truck, and an analysis tab with KPIs, an hours-lost trend and four leaderboards, all at any period from day to year with a delta against the preceding window. Backing it: `TripStop.actual_departure`, `location_name`, and `delay_reason`/`delay_note`/`delay_logged_by`/`delay_logged_at` + a `DelayReason` enum; three `/reports/delays*` endpoints; and an operator alert that fires the moment a stop is reached 30+ min late. **Two real data bugs fixed underneath it**: the mobile/geofence status path never stamped `actual_arrival` at all (and dropoff arrival was backfilled at completion, dating every delivery to when its paperwork finished), so the database could not tell "arrived 2h late" from "arrived on time and waited 2h to unload"; and the mobile operator's Create Trip screen sent no planned times, so every trip booked from a phone was invisible to delay reporting. Reason capture is operator-only by design — drivers already report causes in the WhatsApp group. Verified end to end over real HTTP against a live Postgres, plus a browser walkthrough of the report page on a seeded dataset. Earlier the same day: mobile **operator Home + Drivers list screens rebuilt** on a new `features/dashboard` and `features/drivers` architecture — NativeWind + TanStack Query added to the mobile app for the first time (babel/metro/tailwind config, `QueryClientProvider` in root layout); Home is now AppHeader/context chips/search/scanner, two `DashboardMetricCard`s (Active Trips, Delayed Deliveries), an `ActiveVehiclesSection` carousel (driver+route+status per active trip), and a `DocumentExpirySection` (replaces the old Fleet Utilization block) with real expiring-document counts + a scoped All/Vehicles/Drivers/Company list; Drivers list gained two dark KPI tiles, search/status-filter/sort, and backend-paginated infinite scroll. Then: mobile **operator Driver Details screen** — new `features/drivers` screen at `/operator/driver-details?id=` with profile/statistics, assigned vehicle, contact + licence info, documents, current assignment, performance summary and call/message actions, built on a layered api→services→hooks stack with React Query caching, pull-to-refresh and optimistic status updates; the driver list now taps through to it. Earlier the same day: web dashboard **mobile responsiveness pass**: the fixed 220px sidebar became an off canvas drawer below `lg` driven by a header hamburger, the overflowing 8-item route pill bar is now desktop-only with Quick Create promoted to a header `+`, `DataTable`/`dialog.tsx`/`PageTitle` made phone-friendly, and page containers + stray fixed grids given mobile-first breakpoints. Also fixed a **pre-existing syntax error on `main`** — `handleUpdateStatus` in `DriverListPage.tsx` was missing its closing `};`, which made `tsc -b` fail and would have broken the Docker build/deploy. Previous session, 2026-08-04: fleet data onboarding: Excel export on the Drivers/Vehicles list pages now includes the driver's assigned vehicle plate and the vehicle's assigned driver — backend `getDrivers`/`getVehicles` now `include` the active trip + its vehicle/driver; styled `.xlsx` bulk-entry templates + `docs/MERCON_Fleet_Import_Guide.md` reworked to match those columns. Note: the templates are for **manual collection today — there is no backend import endpoint yet**. Previous session, 2026-08-02: mobile operator UI-kit consistency pass across all 8 operator screens + fixed a duplicate-bottom-nav bug; added real trip lifecycle actions (pickup arrive/verify, delivery verify, replace driver, cancel) to `TripDetailsScreen`; added Driver/Vehicle edit screens; added invoice "Mark as Paid"; added full Customers screen (list/search/create/edit); added an `Operator` "More" hub screen — fixed a real navigation gap where Vehicles/Invoices/VehicleRenewals were only reachable via deep link, not from the UI) · Owner: Hysam (solo dev + AI) · Deadline: ~1 month from July 2026

DriverListPage/VehicleListPage/CustomerListPage/RateCardListPage/LocationListPage/UserManagementPage/InvoiceListPage/DocumentsCenterPage/FleetPerformancePage/RevenueReportsPage/ReportsDashboardPage and all other Details pages were audited and already handle `isError` correctly — no changes needed there. Not done: mobile app was not audited for the same pattern; no live browser verification this pass either (working directory had concurrent uncommitted changes from another active session — some edits to 3 of these files were lost to what looks like a `git reset`/checkout mid-session and had to be silently reapplied and re-verified via grep before commit, so double-check this area if something looks off). **Reliability fixes for intermittent load failures on slow/unstable connections**: added a React `ErrorBoundary` (`components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx`) around the routed outlet in `AppShell.tsx` and the `/login`/`/forgot-password` routes, with a one-time silent auto-reload for stale-chunk (`ChunkLoadError`) failures and a manual Retry screen otherwise — previously a lazy-import failure white-screened the app with no recovery. Bumped the global axios timeout 15s→30s (`lib/api.ts`) and React Query default `retry` 1→2 (`main.tsx`), since the flat 15s timeout was hit by normal-sized heavy list payloads (e.g. trips with `per_page:1000` + nested includes) on slower connections. Fixed `TripListPage`'s KPI queries (`trips-kpi-summary`, `trips-kpi-period`) to toast+retry on failure instead of silently rendering blank cards. Fixed `MonthlyTripsPage`'s monthly board query to show a distinct error+retry state on fetch failure instead of falling through to the generic "no trips this month" empty state (previously indistinguishable from a real empty month — this is the likely cause of "drivers don't show up for some company trips" on bad wifi). Added `onError` fallback+retry UI to the raw `<img>`/`<iframe>` document previews in `OwnerFolderDetail.tsx` and `DocumentPreviewSheet.tsx`, which previously bypassed the axios client entirely and just hung indefinitely with no feedback on a slow network. Added `COMMON_TIMEZONES`/`CommonTimezone` to `@mercon/shared-types` and a `timezone` field on the `Settings`/`PublicSettings` interfaces. Wired backend (`getPublicSettings`/`getSettings`/`updateSettings` in `settingsController.ts`, superadmin-gated same as branding). Added a Timezone dropdown to the web dashboard's Settings → Company Details tab (`SettingsPage.tsx`), backed by `settingsService`. Installed `date-fns-tz` and added `frontend/web-dashboard/src/lib/datetime.ts` (`useDeploymentTimezone`/`formatInDeploymentTz`/`toZonedDisplay`/`zonedInputToUtc`) as the intended single conversion point. `prisma db push` run locally, `tsc --noEmit` clean on both workspaces. **NOT done**: the actual sweep — every existing `date-fns format()`/`toLocaleDateString()`/`toLocaleString()` call across web dashboard (dozens of files: date pickers, trip/invoice/expense tables, exports) and the mobile app still formats in the browser/device's local timezone, not through the new utility; mobile has no timezone conversion utility at all yet. Browser click-through of the new Settings dropdown was not done (port conflict with another running dev server session) — now verified live in-browser too (logged in as admin locally, confirmed `GET /settings/public` returns `timezone: "Asia/Riyadh"` and the Settings → Company Details tab renders the dropdown with all `COMMON_TIMEZONES` options, current value selected correctly) — the local dev DB has no real trip/invoice data seeded, so the *display* sweep below could only be confirmed by typecheck, not visually, this pass. **Follow-up sweep**: converted 37 web-dashboard files (trip/invoice/expense/maintenance/vehicle/driver/customer/document lists, detail pages, Excel/CSV/PDF exports, WhatsApp share text) from `date-fns format()`/`toLocaleDateString()`/`toLocaleString()` (implicit browser-local timezone) to `formatInDeploymentTz`/`useDeploymentTimezone`, so every *display* of a stored timestamp now respects `Settings.timezone`. `tsc --noEmit` clean on both workspaces after the sweep. **NOT done — the write/input side**: date/time *pickers* used for data entry (`TripDateTimeField`, `TripStepRouteTiming`'s truck-arrival field, and the various `<input type="date">`/`type="datetime-local">` fields across trip/invoice/expense/maintenance create-edit forms) still build naive local-wall-clock strings with no explicit timezone, relying on whichever timezone the user's own browser/OS happens to be set to — this only silently matches `Settings.timezone` when the user is physically in that zone. Fixing this properly means reworking these forms to interpret entered wall-clock time as being in the deployment timezone (`zonedInputToUtc` in `src/lib/datetime.ts` exists for this) rather than the browser's — deliberately deferred as separate, higher-risk work since it touches live trip/invoice/expense creation and needs real browser testing, not just a typecheck. Fixed a real bug found along the way: the backend's `vehicleTypeField`/`rateCategoryField` were a hard `z.enum(...)` that silently rejected anything the UI's own "Custom" toggle sent — loosened to free text (`billingTypeField` added the same way), matching how `Trip.rate_category`/`vehicle_type` already worked. Wired billing_type through rate-card create/update/bulk-import/list-filter/lookup, trip create/bulk-import/monthly-board-filter, `importUtils.ts` (`RATE_CARD_COLUMNS`/`TRIP_COLUMNS`), and the Rate Cards / Monthly Trips list filters. Also fixed `TRIP_COLUMNS.vehicle_plate` not recognising a "Vehicle No" header. `prisma db push` run locally; `tsc --noEmit` clean on both `backend/api-server` and `frontend/web-dashboard`. They are read by Gemini Vision, matched to a Driver/Vehicle, and only written to the vault once a human confirms in a review table, so an abandoned import is an unfinished import rather than permanent "Unassigned" clutter. New `documentMatchService` (plate digits / chassis / IQAMA / filename, each match carrying a human-readable reason) is shared by the import and the legacy auto-assign screen, and never auto-creates fleet records — the old path created vehicles named "Vehicle 9973" from unfamiliar plate digits, which is where the junk vehicles came from. OCR is now driven by the configured `DocumentType` catalogue and may answer null instead of defaulting every unrecognised document to `VehicleRegistration` (the cause of so much of the vault displaying as "Vehicle Registration"), so admin-added types are detectable with no code change. Duplicate guard offers Replace / Add-as-extra-file / Skip when a file lands on an already-filled owner+type slot. Review table is ordered worst-first and pre-selects confident matches, so a 50-file drop the AI mostly gets right is a few decisions rather than fifty. Verified against real truck PDFs: FAHAS and Isthimara correctly identified, plate 2541UDA matched to vehicle 2541 at HIGH confidence, and a duplicate Isthimara caught; corrupt files now report their real extraction error as Failed rather than a misleading "needs input". Also removed the Payee/Vendor Name and Employee Name inputs from the modal per owner request; `payee` stays in the payload/schema for legacy records but is no longer editable via the form. Also found and fixed the root cause of a related complaint ("Upload Document" felt broken): `autoAssignUnlinkedDocs` (`bulkOcrController.ts`) looked up a per-vehicle `Folder` row from a `folders` array snapshotted once before its loop, but never appended newly-created folders back into it — so every document auto-assigned to the same not-yet-seen vehicle within one batch run created its own duplicate `Folder` row (confirmed live: one vehicle had 6 duplicate "Vehicle VEH-9973-4127" folders), flooding the Upload modal's Folder dropdown with hundreds of near-identical options. Fixed by pushing each newly-created folder back into the local array; also filtered the Upload modal's general Folder picker (`UploadDocumentModal.tsx`) to exclude `Vehicles`/`Drivers`-category folders entirely, since those owners already have an implicit folder via the owner-folder flow and were never meant to be manually filed into from this picker. Note: existing duplicate `Folder` rows from before this fix are still in the DB and were not backfilled/merged — only new duplicates are prevented. Added an active-trip guard (409 `IN_USE`, same pattern as `locationController.ts`'s) to both single and bulk delete endpoints; deleting a vehicle now clears any driver's `assignedVehicleId` pointing at it, deleting a driver clears its own; new `GET /vehicles/:id/usage` and `GET /drivers/:id/usage` endpoints back real trip/maintenance/expense counts in the delete-confirmation dialogs (replacing the misleading "cannot be undone" copy); new `components/ui/DeletedBadge.tsx` renders a "Deleted" tag next to a referenced vehicle/driver's name wherever `deletedAt` is set. Verified backend `tsc`/frontend `tsc` clean; verified live against dev.mercon.tech's synced DB via direct API calls (create test vehicle+driver+trip, confirm 409 block while `Dispatched`, cancel trip, confirm delete succeeds and reciprocal `assignedVehicleId` clears) and in-browser (Trip Ledger + Trip Details both show the "Deleted" badge next to the now-deleted driver/vehicle).) · Previously (**Documents: paginate Vehicle/Driver folder card sections**: both sections rendered every driver/vehicle folder card at once (31+ vehicles unbounded) instead of a bounded page with a way to see the rest. Updated `TripDetailsPage.tsx` and `tripService.ts` to display full date AND time (`fullDateTime`) alongside creator/editor/deleter `UserChip` components for Created, Updated, and Deleted audit trail pills. `npm run build` verified workspace compilation.) · Previously (**Grant/revoke superadmin**: no way existed to make anyone a superadmin except the seed's `create:` branch — which never touches an existing user, so Mercon's live `admin` account was permanently stuck at `isSuperAdmin: false` with no recovery path short of hand-written SQL. **Backend**: `updateUserBody` (`schemas/index.ts`) gained an optional `isSuperAdmin` boolean; `userController.updateUser` applies it only after a field-level check — re-reads the *requester's* `isSuperAdmin` from the DB (not the route's `authorizeRoles('Admin')`, which would let any Admin self-escalate) and 403s `FORBIDDEN` if they're not already a superadmin. Revoking is blocked with `409 LAST_SUPERADMIN` if it would leave zero superadmins on the deployment — same spirit as the existing "can't delete your own account" guard on `deleteUser`. `getUsers`/`createUser`/`updateUser` responses now include `isSuperAdmin` so the UI can render it. **Frontend**: `UserModal.tsx` gained a superadmin checkbox, shown only when the viewer is themselves a superadmin (`canManageSuperAdmin` prop, threaded from `UserManagementPage.tsx`'s `authStore.getUser()`) editing an existing Admin-role user — never offered at creation, so a new account is never superadmin by default; `UserManagementPage.tsx`'s role column gained a small "Superadmin" pill next to the role badge. **Production fix**: `backend/api-server/prisma/grant-superadmin.sql` — a one-time, idempotent `UPDATE "User" SET "isSuperAdmin" = true WHERE username = 'admin'` for the owner to run once against the live database (mirrors `cleanup-fake-seed-data.sql`'s pattern); once that's run, granting it to anyone else goes through the UI above, no more manual SQL needed. Verified end-to-end against a locally running API: a plain Operator gets 403 at the route level; a non-superadmin Admin gets 403 specifically on the `isSuperAdmin` field but can still edit other fields normally; a superadmin can grant it to another Admin and later revoke their own once someone else holds it; revoking the last remaining superadmin is blocked with `409`; the SQL statement itself executed and confirmed idempotent on a second run. `tsc --noEmit` clean on backend and frontend, `vite build` clean.) · Previously (**Logo upload for deployment branding**: the superadmin branding form's "Logo URL" field was paste-only — no actual upload path, contradicting the original plan to reuse the existing generic upload endpoint. `SettingsPage.tsx`'s Company Details tab now has a real upload button (superadmin-only) that posts to the existing `POST /upload` (`uploadRoutes.ts`, already Admin/Operator-gated, images-or-PDF filter, 50MB limit — no new backend endpoint needed) and drops the returned `file_url` into the form; the URL field stays editable too, for a superadmin who'd rather point at an externally-hosted image. A small logo preview renders next to the field so what's about to be saved is visible before hitting Save. `settingsService.uploadLogo()` wraps the multipart POST. Actually persisting the URL still goes through the existing `requireSuperAdmin`-gated `PUT /settings` — the upload endpoint itself only stores a file, it doesn't touch Settings, so a non-superadmin Admin/Operator could upload a file but still can't make it the deployment's logo (verified: PUT /settings with a crafted logoUrl 403s for a plain Admin). Verified end-to-end against a locally running API: uploaded a real PNG, saved it as `Settings.logoUrl`, confirmed it comes back through the unauthenticated `GET /settings/public` (what the login page reads pre-auth) and that the file itself resolves at its `/uploads/...` URL with the correct content-type. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `vite build` clean.) · Previously (**Backend enforcement for module toggles**: the `Settings.enabledModules` toggle built for multi-client productization was frontend-only — `Sidebar.tsx`/`RequireModule.tsx` hid nav and blocked routes, but `requireModuleEnabled` (`middlewares/rbac.ts`) was applied to zero backend routes, so e.g. `GET /api/invoices` kept serving data with "Invoices" toggled off. `CLAUDE.md` requires both-layer enforcement; this closes the gap. **Fixed a fail-closed blocker found while wiring this up**: `getOrCreateSettings()` created the Settings row with no `enabledModules`, defaulting to `[]` — since `requireModuleEnabled` fails closed, any deployment where a trip was created before the seed ran would 403 every gated module at once. Now creates with `[...MODULE_KEYS]`. **`MODULE_KEYS` (`packages/shared-types`) drops from 8 to 6** — `locations` and `rate-cards` are no longer toggleable, moved to core: disabling them 403'd the trip wizard's location picker/rate lookup even though trip creation itself doesn't depend on the HTTP routes (locations are auto-created in-process by `resolveLocation()`, rate cards looked up via `findRateForLane()`). **Backend**: `requireModuleEnabled` applied to `invoiceRoutes`/`expenseRoutes`/`maintenanceRoutes`/`reportsRoutes`/`documentRoutes`/`folderRoutes`/`trashRoutes` (gating inside the router, not at the `index.ts` mount point, so it covers both the `/api` and root mounts of the same router instance); per-route gates added to the invoice-creation aliases living on `tripRoutes.ts` (`/trips/:id/mark-invoiced`/`unmark-invoiced`) and to `GET /mobile/documents`; `reportsController.ts`/`delayReportsController.ts` now null out (not zero — 0 would read as "confirmed zero" rather than "not tracked here") invoice/maintenance-derived fields in `getSummary`/`getFleetPerformance`/`getCustomReport`/delay analysis when their module is off, so the dashboard keeps loading instead of 403ing outright; `vehicleController.ts`'s `getVehicleFinancials`/`getFleetFinancials` (the "Vehicle P&L" endpoints, core nav with no toggle of their own) do the same for their invoice/maintenance-derived numbers; `trashController.ts` filters listed items and rejects restore/hard-delete by module (`RateCard` stays exempt — now core). New shared `getEnabledModules()` in `settingsController.ts` is the one source of truth `requireModuleEnabled` and every controller above reads from, so they can't drift. **Frontend**: `lib/api.ts`'s response interceptor now redirects home on a `MODULE_DISABLED` 403 (covers a tab left open on a gated page across a superadmin's live toggle — normal navigation was already blocked client-side); `reportsService.ts` types updated to reflect the now-nullable summary fields. **Found and fixed in passing**: `VehicleFinancialsPage.tsx`'s Excel/PDF export functions called `.toLocaleString()` directly on `net_profit`/`total_expenses`/`margin_percent` — would have thrown on `null` the moment maintenance is disabled; guarded with `?? 0`. Deliberately **not** touched: nested `invoices`/`maintenanceRecords`/`documents` includes on `getTripById`, vehicle/driver detail responses — stripping those touches core read paths for data the viewing company already owns, flagged as a non-goal rather than attempted. Also flagged, not fixed: `thirdPartyRoutes.ts` has no `authenticateJWT`/`authorizeRoles` at all — pre-existing hole, unrelated to modules. Verified end-to-end against a locally running API: disabling invoices 403'd `/api/invoices`, `/invoices` (root mount), `/api/reports/revenue`, and the trip mark-invoiced alias; `/api/reports/summary` stayed 200 with `revenue_this_month`/`monthly_revenue_chart` null; `/api/trips`, `/api/locations`, `/api/rate-cards` stayed 200 throughout; trash restore of a disabled-module type 403'd; re-enabling took effect on the very next request with the same still-valid JWT; deleting the Settings row and hitting a gated route recreated it with every module enabled rather than 403ing everything. `tsc --noEmit` clean on backend and frontend, `vite build` clean.) · Previously (**Custom Company Reports Generator (`/company-reports`)**: Replaced the broken `/reports/client-templates` prototype (which lost all fonts/fills/borders on export — `customTemplateExport.ts` copied cell styles from the sample data row *after* erasing that same row) with a byte-exact engine. New approach: `.xlsx` is unzipped, only the target worksheet's `<sheetData>` rows are spliced (values injected, style `s=` indices preserved from the cloned band row, formulas/merges/mergeCell/conditionalFormatting/dataValidation/dimension refs shifted by the row-count delta), and every other ZIP part (styles.xml, theme, drawings, charts, other sheets) is re-zipped byte-identical to the upload — verified by a unit test asserting exact byte equality against a real customer fixture (`docs/templates/COMPANY QUOTATION (1).xlsx`, which has JDL/IMILE/SHIPA/ARKAN/AKS sheets). Backend: `services/reports/xlsxTemplate/{splice,inspect,tripReportData,aliases,xmlRowSplice}.ts` (new deps `exceljs` read-only for inspection, `fflate` for the ZIP), new `ReportTemplate` Prisma model (file bytes + confirmed layout stored in Postgres, not the ephemeral `/tmp` uploads volume), new `/report-templates` routes (`inspect`, CRUD, `:id/preview`, `:id/generate`) gated by a new `company-reports` module key (with a seed backfill so the existing mercon.tech Settings row picks it up). Also fixed 3 underlying data bugs in `getCustomReport`/the new `fetchTripRows` service: date filtering on `createdAt` instead of the trip's own date, a hard 500-row cap, and `receiver` stubbed as `"Dropoff Stop N"` instead of the real `TripStop.location_name`. Frontend: new `CompanyReportsGeneratorPage.tsx` + `TemplateMappingEditor.tsx` (the human-confirms-the-mapping step the old page never had — `customMapping` existed in the engine but no UI ever passed it), `reportTemplateService.ts`. Wired as its own top-level sidebar item (not a Reports sub-tab) at `/company-reports`; `/reports/client-templates` redirects there. Removed `CompanyReportTemplatesPage.tsx` and `customTemplateExport.ts`. Hardened against two real customer files (a JDL trip-confirmation and an IMILE monthly invoice) which exposed four defects the synthetic fixture missed: (1) only the style-band row was replaced, so the customer's own sample rows survived underneath the generated data — `TemplateLayout` now carries an explicit, operator-editable `dataEndRow`; (2) shared formulas (`<f t="shared" si="N"/>`, which carry no formula text) cloned as empty cells, silently blanking the VAT/Total columns — formulas are now resolved against their master and emitted standalone; (3) a totals row's `SUM(M1:M37)` had its header-anchored start dragged down to `M14`, under-reporting the invoice total — row-shifting now distinguishes copy semantics (cloning, `$` pinned) from insert semantics (trailing rows, only refs at/below the block move); (4) the stale `calcChain.xml` cache was shipped pointing at moved cells, the usual cause of Excel's "we found a problem with some content" prompt — it is now dropped with its content-type override and relationship. Also fixed token substitution (banner tokens live in `sharedStrings.xml`, not inline in the sheet) and band detection (read a non-existent `style.id`). Verified: `npm run build` (root) succeeds; `npm run test:xlsx` (9 tests) passes; both real customer files generate 25-row reports that load cleanly in `openpyxl` (an implementation independent of the one used to read them) with **zero style mismatches** across every generated row, correct de-shared formulas, dates round-tripping as real dates, and UTF-8 (CJK) text intact; `prisma db push` + seed run twice against a local Postgres confirm the schema applies cleanly and the module-key backfill is idempotent; full API round-trip (login → inspect real fixture → create template → preview → generate) tested against a local server, output re-parses cleanly with ExcelJS. Added a rate-category filter (`Trip.rate_category`, e.g. "Trip", "Monthly Round", "Extra Trip/Round Trip") to preview/generate — real customer workbooks split trip-rate vs monthly-rate business into separate sheets (an IMILE file has an "extra" sheet), so a saved template needs to pull only its own slice rather than every trip for the customer. Verified end-to-end against a local Postgres: saved two independently-named templates ("IMILE Trip Monthly", "IMILE Extra") under the same customer with no collision, and each template's preview/generate correctly returns only its own rate-category slice of trips. · Same day, in parallel (**Smart Report Builder bugfixes**: post-review pass on the report builder found and fixed 3 defects — (1) `frontend/web-dashboard/src/router.tsx` referenced `moduleKey="locations"`/`"rate-cards"` on `<RequireModule>`, which no longer typecheck since a prior commit removed those two keys from `MODULE_KEYS` (they became always-on core infra) — **this broke `npm run build`**; fixed by dropping the two `RequireModule` wrappers to match the "core, not toggleable" intent. (2) `reportEngine/derived.ts`'s `tripIncome()` read `t.invoices[0]` unconditionally, but `reportEngine/resolver.ts` only includes the `invoices` relation when a spec explicitly references an `invoices.*` field — since `trips.revenue` is the default Values field in the Advanced Builder, every Quick Report preset, both landing-page templates, and the Ask Mercon parser, this crashed the query endpoint on the feature's own golden path; fixed by making `invoices` optional (`t.invoices?.[0]`). (3) `resolver.ts`'s row/column group-key join used a literal NUL byte as separator, making the file register as binary to tooling; replaced with `'||'`. **Not fixed in this pass** (flagged, needs an explicit decision): `requireModuleEnabled` was added to 7 previously-ungated route files (`reports`, `documents`, `folders`, `invoices`, `expenses`, `maintenance`, `trash`) in the same commit as the report builder work; `Settings.enabledModules` defaults to `[]` at the DB level and is only backfilled to the full list on **row creation** (`seed.ts`, `update: {}`) — if production's `Settings` singleton row already exists with an empty `enabledModules`, this 403s all 7 modules' APIs the moment it deploys. Needs a production data check (or an explicit backfill) before/after this lands, independent of the report builder itself. Built a complete, testable ad-hoc reporting system with a real backend Prisma query engine and drag-and-drop pivot table builder. **Backend**: `SavedReport` & `ScheduledReport` Prisma models (`npx prisma db push --accept-data-loss`), `reportEngine/schema.ts` (10 modules, canonical columns, join graph), `reportEngine/derived.ts` (shared `tripIncome`/`isEarned`), `reportEngine/resolver.ts` (`runReportQuery` with 400 injection guards, 1-hop relation include, JS group/agg/filter, KPI computation), `reportBuilderController.ts` & `reportBuilderRoutes.ts` with Zod validation (`reportQuerySpecBody`, `createSavedReportBody`, etc.) mounted at `/report-builder/*`. **Frontend**: `@dnd-kit/core` installed, `reportBuilderService.ts`, `askMerconParser.ts` (rule-based NL→spec parser), `ReportBuilderLandingPage.tsx` (top bar layout matching MERCON guidelines with scope pill `[ 🏢 MERCON Logistics ↕ ]` & category badge, 3 entry cards, 4 pre-built templates, Saved Reports & Scheduled Deliveries tabs), `QuickReportPage.tsx` (3-step wizard), `AdvancedBuilderPage.tsx` (3-panel workspace: expandable data panel, droppable structure zones for Rows/Columns/Values/Filters, auto-connect badge, FilterBuilder, live preview, KPI cards, Recharts view switcher: Table/Bar/Line/Pie/Area), `SaveReportModal.tsx`, `ScheduleReportModal.tsx`, CSV/Excel/PDF export. Mounted at `/report-builder` and registered in `Sidebar.tsx` under COMPLIANCE. Verified: backend `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, frontend `npm run build` clean.) · Previously (**Dedicated Company Excel Reports Page (`/reports/client-templates`)**: Built a dedicated page for generating customer-specific company Excel trip reports matching client format requirements (e.g. ARKAN, SHIPA, JDL). Features: Company Selector, Date Horizon & Status filters, Drag-and-Drop Excel Uploader, Presets Manager (save/load company templates locally), 4-Card Instrument Panel KPIs, Sheet Inspector (header row, sheet name, column auto-mapping), and Live Trip Ledger Preview table. Fixed a real bug found in passing: `authService.ts`'s dev-only mock-login fallback had no `import.meta.env.DEV` guard, so a 5xx/network error in production silently granted a fake client-side Admin session. **Web dashboard**: de-branding codemod converted ~700 hardcoded `#E8450F`-family Tailwind arbitrary values (`bg-[#E8450F]` etc., 102 files) to the `bg-brand`/`text-brand`/... tokens already registered in `index.css`'s `@theme inline` block, plus the remaining on-screen literals (spinners, map pins, chart fills) to `var(--color-brand)`; new `useBranding`/`useApplyBranding` hooks fetch `/settings/public` and write it to the CSS vars + `document.title` at boot (login page included, pre-auth); new `BrandLogo` component replaces hardcoded `<img src="/mercon-logo.png">` in Sidebar/LoginPage with a Settings-driven `logoUrl` (falls back to the existing PNGs); `Sidebar.tsx` nav items gained an optional `moduleKey` filtered against `Settings.enabledModules`; new `RequireSuperAdmin`/`RequireModule` route guards (same pattern as existing `RequireRole`) applied to the 8 optional-module routes; `SettingsPage.tsx`'s previously-decorative "Company Details" tab is now a real superadmin-only branding/module-toggle form. Verified: backend `tsc --noEmit` clean, frontend `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean, mobile `tsc --noEmit` clean, `docker compose config` resolves without error, seed re-run twice against local dev DB confirmed idempotent (no fake data, `isSuperAdmin` set once and not overwritten), full settings CRUD + superadmin-revocation-takes-effect-without-relogin tested end-to-end against a locally running API.) · Previously (**Expenses Module** (`/expenses`, new page): salaries, fuel, rent, and every other operating cost had nowhere to be recorded — MaintenanceRecord only covers vehicle service orders, and Invoice only covers customer billing, so there was no way to log money going out. **Database & Backend** (`schema.prisma`, `expenseController.ts`, `expenseRoutes.ts`, `trashController.ts`): added `Expense` model (free-text `category` e.g. Salary/Fuel/Rent, `status` Paid/Pending, optional `driverId`/`vehicleId` links, `amount`/`currency`/`expense_date`/`payee`/`payment_method`/`description`, standard ref_id + audit/soft-delete columns) with back-relations on `Driver`/`Vehicle`; registered `GET/POST /expenses`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /expenses/:id` gated to Admin/Operator via `authorizeRoles`; wired into the recycle bin (restore/hard-delete). **Shared Types** (`packages/shared-types`): added `EXPENSE_CATEGORIES`/`EXPENSE_PAYMENT_METHODS` constants (free-text columns, not Postgres enums, same pattern as `VEHICLE_TYPES`). **Frontend Web Dashboard**: `expenseService.ts`, `ExpenseListPage.tsx` (KPI cards for total/paid/pending/salary amounts, filterable/searchable `DataTable` ledger, CSV export), `ExpenseModal.tsx` (create/edit form with category, status, optional driver/vehicle link, amount, payment method, payee, notes); added to the sidebar FINANCE group and the router at `/expenses`. No seed data added (owner rule: never seed fake financial records).
